# Alpaca Bot 0.6.0 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development to implement this plan task by task (Carmelo chose SDD, all Opus, on 2026-09-15). Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Ship Alpaca Bot 0.6.0: the four 0.5 audit follow-ups and the quality tickets first, then a chat drawer on every admin screen with a Settings › Access tab, an abilities toolkit, and the plugin half of a remote MCP client.

**Architecture:**
- Hardening lands first. A new `AlpacaBot\Access` resolver turns one stored capability per row into the default the existing `alpaca_bot/capability/*` filters receive, so code still wins. The tool floor, the settings split and the shortcode row sit on it. web_fetch pins the checked address into cURL, and the MCP client gets a Symfony client pinned the same way.
- The drawer reuses the chat shell through `GET /view/panel`: an `admin_footer` drawer on classic screens, a `PluginSidebar` in the block editor.
- The MCP client itself is php-agents 0.16 (Kanboard project 79). This plan builds against a seam with a test double and swaps in the real client in Task 28.

**Tech Stack:** PHP 8.4, WordPress 6.9–7.1, php-agents (Strauss-prefixed), htmx 2.0, TypeScript and esbuild via pnpm, Pest with Brain Monkey, node --test, Playwright with wp-env.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-15-alpaca-bot-0-6.md`. The wayfinding map is Kanboard #4364; decisions are on its closed cruxes #4365–#4372.

## Global Constraints

- **Versioning:** 0.x only; never write "1.0" in code, docs, commits or ticket titles. The integration branch is `develop`, and it merges to `main` only at the release (Task 30). Version markers and `readme.txt`'s header block change only in Task 30.
- **Floors:** PHP 8.4, WordPress ≥ 6.9, tested to 7.1. Guard every 7.1-only API with `function_exists` or a version check.
- **Models:** all Opus for now: implementer, fixer and reviewer (task review and whole-branch review). No Fable; never Haiku or Sonnet. Pass `model: "opus"` explicitly on every dispatch; an omitted model inherits the session's and defeats the rule. The ticket carries `implementer_model: opus` and `reviewer_model: opus`.
- **TDD:** write the failing test, run it and see it fail, implement the minimum, run it and see it pass, commit. There are no untested behaviour changes.
- **Gates** (run before a task reports DONE, and report their tails):
  - `composer check`, `bin/test-integration.sh`, `pnpm check`, `pnpm e2e` (or `pnpm e2e:offline` where wp-env isn't needed);
  - `composer docs:hooks` and `composer docs:readme`, each with its no-diff check;
  - `bash bin/build-zip.sh` whenever shipped files change.
- **Dependencies:** no new ones, except the DOM devDependency in Task 15 and php-agents `~0.16.0` in Task 28. Each comes only after `/powerup:supply-chain` passes; stop and ask Carmelo if it doesn't.
- **Commits:** commit only the paths you changed (`git add <path>…`, never `-A`/`-a`). Conventional lower-case subjects in house style (`feat(access): …`, `fix(toolkit): …`, `test: …`, `docs: …`). No attribution trailers. Implementers don't push; the controller does.
- **Browser checks:** `node ~/Projects/wp-harness/bin/wph.js shot alpaca10 <path> …`. Never the Claude in-app browser or Claude in Chrome on `*.wp.test`. Never touch `alpacabot.wp.test` (the 0.4 demo). After editing `resources/`, run `pnpm build`. Coordinate before remounting `alpaca10`; other sessions use it.
- **Board:** Kanboard project 17. Never set `approved_by` metadata and never run `kanboard-run-milestone.sh`; either one arms a watcher that launches headless workers. Log time by hand in comments. Ticket ids are Kanboard tasks, never GitHub issues.
- **Named review target: false load-bearing comments** (Kanboard #2978 comment 637). This codebase's docblocks carry the *argument*, so a false reason is a real defect. Every reviewer brief names both mechanisms:
  1. a sentence that argues from a counterfactual about the rejected alternative, or claims a completeness it doesn't have ("its one caller", "every X goes through", "the only Z");
  2. a sentence that was true when written and is silently falsified by a later change without being edited, often in a file nobody had reason to open.

  Reviewers treat each load-bearing comment as a claim to verify against the file, a command run, or upstream source at its pinned version. When implementers and fixers add a mechanism, they re-read every nearby sentence that was true before, and they list in their report the sentences they re-checked and any they corrected in their own drafts. 0.6 deliberately falsifies several shipped sentences ("a later 0.x release", the egress-policy disclosure, "the only capability gate over the tools"). The task that falsifies one rewrites it.
- **Execution order:** phases run in order A → G.
  - **Phase F (Tasks 24–27)** needs only the seam, so it can run in parallel with Phases D–E once the php-agents contract is frozen on Kanboard #4364.
  - **Task 28** is blocked until php-agents `v0.16.0` is tagged.
  - **Task 30** tags only on Carmelo's direct word; a relayed approval from another session is not his go.

---
## Phase A: Groundwork

Five tasks that change no public behaviour except #4327's error wording, and that later phases build on: two classes out of `Rest\`, a suite that can run on a runner with no provider, the error prefix, the migration bound, and the faked-tool-call helper Task 16 streams through.

### Task 1: Move `Rest\Errors` and `Rest\RateLimit` to `AlpacaBot\Errors` and `AlpacaBot\RateLimit`

**Files:**
- Create (by `git mv`): `src/Errors.php` (from `src/Rest/Errors.php`), `src/RateLimit.php` (from `src/Rest/RateLimit.php`)
- Create (by `git mv`): `tests/Unit/ErrorsTest.php` (from `tests/Unit/Rest/ErrorsTest.php`), `tests/Unit/RateLimitTest.php` (from `tests/Unit/Rest/RateLimitTest.php`)
- Create: `tests/Unit/NamespaceTest.php`
- Modify (add `use AlpacaBot\Errors;`, which they used unqualified inside `Rest\`): `src/Rest/ChatController.php`, `src/Rest/ConversationsController.php`, `src/Rest/Controller.php`, `src/Rest/ModelsController.php`, `src/Rest/SettingsController.php`, `src/Rest/StreamController.php`, `src/Rest/UsageController.php`, `src/Rest/ViewController.php`
- Modify (add `use AlpacaBot\RateLimit;`): `src/Rest/Controller.php` (`:122`), `src/Rest/StreamBudget.php` (`:237`, `:263`)
- Modify (`use` lines and docblocks): `src/Shortcodes/Chat.php:12-13,64,68`, `src/Abilities/Register.php:8-9,58,99`, `src/Toolkit/SummarizeToolkit.php:8,74`
- Modify (docblocks only): `src/Shortcodes/AgentShim.php:96`, `src/Provider/WpAi/CoreClient.php:244`, `src/Chat/Pipeline.php:499`
- Modify (comments only): `tests/Unit/Shortcodes/ChatTest.php:293,364`, `tests/Unit/Toolkit/SummarizeToolkitTest.php:77`, `tests/Integration/ShortcodesTest.php:140`, `tests/stubs/wp-rest.php:6`, `phpcs.xml.dist:62,104`
- Regenerate: `docs/hooks.md` (row `alpaca_bot/rate_limit`, source column `src/Rest/RateLimit.php:82` becomes `src/RateLimit.php:82`)
- Test: `tests/Unit/ErrorsTest.php`, `tests/Unit/RateLimitTest.php`, `tests/Unit/NamespaceTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces (same public API as today; only the namespace moves):
  - `final class AlpacaBot\Errors`:
    - `forbidden(string $message = ''): \WP_Error`
    - `tooMany(int $retryAfter): \WP_Error`
    - `capExceeded(Chat\CapExceeded $e): \WP_Error`
    - `badRequest(string $message): \WP_Error`
    - `methodNotAllowed(): \WP_Error`
    - `streamTimeout(int $seconds): \WP_Error`
    - `notFound(string $what): \WP_Error`
    - `provider(\Throwable $e): \WP_Error`
    - `fromPipeline(\Throwable $e): \WP_Error`

    All of these are `public static`. Task 11 adds its concurrency refusal here.
  - `final class AlpacaBot\RateLimit`:
    - `public const PER_MINUTE = 30`
    - `public function hit(int $userId, string $bucket = 'chat'): array{allowed: bool, remaining: int, retry_after: int}`
    - `public static function subject(int $userId): string`

**No deprecated aliases.** Both classes are `final` internals. The public documents are `docs/api.md` and `docs/hooks.md`:
- `docs/api.md` names neither class. It names only the error codes, and `ErrorsTest` in prose at §5, whose name doesn't change.
- `docs/hooks.md` names `src/Rest/RateLimit.php:82` only in its generated source column, which `composer docs:hooks` rewrites.
- `README.md` and `readme.txt` name neither. Checked: `git grep -n -e 'Rest\\Errors' -e 'Rest\\RateLimit' -e 'Rest/Errors' -e 'Rest/RateLimit'` outside `docs/superpowers`, `docs/reviews` and `docs/research`, which are dated records and are left as they were written.

A `class_alias` would keep a name nobody was promised.

**Precedent for the scan test:** tests already read source files to pin a fact about them. `tests/Unit/Admin/AssetsTest.php:204-206` reads `resources/css/alpaca-bot-shortcode.css` and `build.mjs`, and `tests/Unit/Admin/MenuTest.php:20,32` reads `assets/img/menu-icon.svg`. `tests/Unit/HooksDocTest.php` runs `bin/hooks-doc.php` over the real `src/`. PHPStan already catches a stale `use`. What nothing catches is a stale docblock, which is the house style's failure mode (mechanism 2), and there are more docblock mentions below than code ones.

Move the two tests and point them at the new names. The only edit to each is the `use` line.

```bash
git mv tests/Unit/Rest/ErrorsTest.php tests/Unit/ErrorsTest.php
git mv tests/Unit/Rest/RateLimitTest.php tests/Unit/RateLimitTest.php
```
In `tests/Unit/ErrorsTest.php`, replace `use AlpacaBot\Rest\Errors;` with `use AlpacaBot\Errors;`. In `tests/Unit/RateLimitTest.php`, replace `use AlpacaBot\Rest\RateLimit;` with `use AlpacaBot\RateLimit;`.

Create `tests/Unit/NamespaceTest.php`:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use AlpacaBot\Errors;
use AlpacaBot\RateLimit;

/*
 * Errors and RateLimit left Rest\ in 0.6: Shortcodes\Chat and Abilities\Register use both, and
 * Toolkit\SummarizeToolkit uses Errors, none of them from a route. PHPStan catches a stale
 * `use`; nothing else catches a stale docblock, and in this codebase the docblock carries the
 * argument. The scan covers what ships and what documents the plugin; the dated records under
 * docs/superpowers, docs/reviews and docs/research say what was true when they were written and
 * are not scanned.
 */

it('autoloads Errors and RateLimit from the plugin root namespace, and the Rest\ names are gone', function (): void {
    expect(class_exists(Errors::class))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(class_exists(RateLimit::class))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(class_exists('AlpacaBot\Rest\Errors'))->toBeFalse()
        ->and(class_exists('AlpacaBot\Rest\RateLimit'))->toBeFalse();
});

it('leaves no file that ships or documents the plugin naming Rest\Errors or Rest\RateLimit', function (): void {
    $root = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
    $files = ['docs/api.md', 'docs/hooks.md', 'phpcs.xml.dist', 'README.md', 'readme.txt'];
    foreach (['src', 'tests', 'bin'] as $dir) {
        $iterator = new RecursiveIteratorIterator(new RecursiveDirectoryIterator($root . '/' . $dir, FilesystemIterator::SKIP_DOTS));
        foreach ($iterator as $file) {
            if ($file instanceof SplFileInfo && $file->isFile() && in_array($file->getExtension(), ['php', 'ts', 'md', 'sh', 'txt'], true)) {
                $files[] = substr($file->getPathname(), strlen($root) + 1);
            }
        }
    }
    $stale = [];
    foreach ($files as $path) {
        if ($path === 'tests/Unit/NamespaceTest.php') {
            continue;
        }
        foreach (preg_split('~\R~', (string) file_get_contents($root . '/' . $path)) ?: [] as $n => $line) {
            if (preg_match('~Rest[\\\\/](?:Errors|RateLimit)\b~', $line) === 1) {
                $stale[] = $path . ':' . ($n + 1);
            }
        }
    }
    expect($stale)->toBe([]);
});
```

### Task 2: Hermetic integration suite and no `[#2977]` probes (Kanboard #4322)

**Most of the ticket already landed on this branch**, in `d46a096` ("test(integration): make the suite portable and its output only results", 2026-09-09, an ancestor of `HEAD`):
- **The probes.** It removed all six `[#2977]` `fwrite(STDERR, …)` probes from `tests/Integration/ConversationStoreTest.php`; the ticket counted two. `git grep -n '\[#2977\]' -- tests` is empty today.
- **The DNS failure.** It showed the `Provider error: Could not resolve host: ollama.internal` line was never a network call. It was fixture text that `ShortcodesTest` handed `fakeProvider()`, logged by `Errors::provider()` under `WP_DEBUG`. It moved that fixture and its siblings to the RFC 6761 `.invalid` TLD: `tests/Integration/ShortcodesTest.php:150`, `ChatRoutesTest.php:134` and `StreamRoutesTest.php:165` all say `ollama-gateway.invalid`.

The ticket is still open (column 73), and one real gap is left.

**The gap: a test that does not fake the provider still reaches the network, silently.**
- `Provider\Factory::ollama()` builds `OllamaProvider` on the Strauss-prefixed Symfony `HttpClient` (`src/Provider/Factory.php:10-11,126,144`), not `WP_Http`, so no `pre_http_request` stub can see it.
- `ModelCatalog::discover()` forgives the provider's failure (`src/Provider/ModelCatalog.php:88-94`, `catch (\Throwable) { $models = []; }`). So a test that touches the catalog without `TestCase::fakeProvider()` makes a real connection attempt to the stored `provider.base_url` and hears nothing back. That URL is:
  - `http://localhost:11434/v1` by default (`src/Settings/Schema.php:73`);
  - `http://ollama.internal:11434/v1` in `SettingsPageTest.php:160` and `UsageRetentionTest.php:60`.
- `HelpTabsTest.php:68` constructs the real `ModelCatalog`, and 6 of the 17 test files never call `fakeProvider()` (`grep -c "fakeProvider(" tests/Integration/*.php`).

The fix makes "no network" a property of the suite, not of each test's care. The shared `tests/Integration/bootstrap.php` (both `WPH_MODE`s load it, `bin/test-integration.sh:7-8`) gets two guards:
- **`WP_Http`.** A last-priority `pre_http_request` refuses any request no test stubbed, with an error naming the URL. A test's own stub at priority 10 answers first, as `ToolkitsTest.php:141,165` and `ShortcodesTest.php:343,370` already do.
- **The provider.** A first-priority `alpaca_bot/provider` swaps a configured `OllamaProvider` for `OfflineProvider`, whose every call throws. `fakeProvider()` (`TestCase.php:66`, default priority) still replaces it, and a `WpAiClientProvider` is left alone: `WpAiClientTest.php:272` asserts `Factory::make()` returns one, and its core provider is an in-test fake.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/Integration/OfflineProvider.php`
- Create: `tests/Integration/HermeticTest.php`
- Modify: `tests/Integration/bootstrap.php` (two `tests_add_filter()` calls, before `require $tests . '/includes/bootstrap.php';`)
- Modify: `tests/Integration/SettingsPageTest.php:160` and `tests/Integration/UsageRetentionTest.php:60,66` (`ollama.internal` becomes `ollama.invalid`, the `.invalid` rule `d46a096` set)
- Modify: `bin/test-integration.sh` (header comment: one paragraph saying the suite refuses the network itself)
- Test: `tests/Integration/HermeticTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces:
  - `final class AlpacaBot\Tests\Integration\OfflineProvider implements ProviderInterface`, with `public const MESSAGE`.
  - The `WP_Error` code `alpaca_bot_tests_offline`, for any unstubbed `WP_Http` request in the integration suite.
  - Later tasks' integration tests (Task 12 web_fetch, Task 14 egress, Tasks 22 and 26–27) must stub `pre_http_request` or inject their resolver or client. The guard does not cover `Mcp\Egress`'s Symfony client, which Task 14 makes injectable for exactly this reason.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/Integration/OfflineProvider.php`. The test names it, so it comes first and the failure is the guard's absence, not a missing class:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace AlpacaBot\Tests\Integration;

use AlpacaBot\Vendor\CarmeloSantana\PHPAgents\Contract\ProviderInterface;
use AlpacaBot\Vendor\CarmeloSantana\PHPAgents\Provider\Response;

/**
 * The model provider an integration test gets unless it asks for TestCase::fakeProvider()
 * (Kanboard #4322). bootstrap.php swaps it in for the configured Ollama provider, which talks
 * to the network through Symfony's HttpClient, where no `pre_http_request` stub can reach it.
 * Every call throws, so a test that meant to fake the provider and forgot fails where it calls
 * it, and one that only touches the catalog on the way (ModelCatalog forgives a provider that
 * throws, as it forgives one that is down) gets the empty catalog an unreachable host would give,
 * without a connection attempt behind it.
 */
final class OfflineProvider implements ProviderInterface
{
    public const MESSAGE = 'The integration suite has no model provider: call TestCase::fakeProvider() in this test.';

    public function chat(array $messages, array $tools = [], array $options = []): Response
    {
        throw new \RuntimeException(self::MESSAGE);
    }

    public function stream(array $messages, array $tools = [], array $options = []): iterable
    {
        throw new \RuntimeException(self::MESSAGE);
    }

    public function structured(array $messages, string $schema, array $options = []): mixed
    {
        throw new \RuntimeException(self::MESSAGE);
    }

    public function models(): array
    {
        throw new \RuntimeException(self::MESSAGE);
    }

    public function isAvailable(): bool
    {
        return false;
    }

    public function getModel(): string
    {
        return '';
    }

    public function withModel(string $model): static
    {
        return $this;
    }
}
```
Create `tests/Integration/HermeticTest.php`:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace AlpacaBot\Tests\Integration;

use AlpacaBot\Plugin;
use AlpacaBot\Provider\Factory;
use AlpacaBot\Settings\Store;

/**
 * The suite refuses the network itself (Kanboard #4322), so a pass needs none: on a GitHub
 * runner under wp-env, on the harness, or behind a dead proxy. Two doors, one guard each, both
 * in bootstrap.php: WP_Http, which a test opens with its own `pre_http_request` stub, and the
 * configured model provider, which a test opens with fakeProvider().
 */
final class HermeticTest extends TestCase
{
    public function test_a_request_no_test_stubbed_is_refused_before_it_leaves_the_process(): void
    {
        $response = wp_remote_get('https://example.com/');

        $this->assertWPError($response);
        $this->assertSame('alpaca_bot_tests_offline', $response->get_error_code());
        $this->assertStringContainsString('https://example.com/', $response->get_error_message());
    }

    public function test_a_stub_the_test_adds_still_answers(): void
    {
        add_filter('pre_http_request', static fn(): array => [
            'headers' => [],
            'body' => 'stubbed',
            'response' => ['code' => 200, 'message' => 'OK'],
            'cookies' => [],
            'filename' => null,
        ]);

        $this->assertSame('stubbed', wp_remote_retrieve_body(wp_remote_get('https://example.com/')));
    }

    public function test_the_configured_provider_is_offline_until_the_test_fakes_one(): void
    {
        Plugin::instance()->get(Store::class)->set('provider.base_url', 'http://ollama.invalid:11434/v1');
        $provider = Plugin::instance()->get(Factory::class)->make('any-model');
        $this->assertInstanceOf(OfflineProvider::class, $provider);
        try {
            $provider->models();
            $this->fail('the offline provider answered');
        } catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
            $this->assertSame(OfflineProvider::MESSAGE, $e->getMessage());
        }

        $this->fakeProvider();
        $this->assertSame('fake-model', Plugin::instance()->get(Factory::class)->make('any-model')->getModel());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it and see it fail**

Run: `bin/test-integration.sh --filter HermeticTest`

```php
// No test reaches the network (Kanboard #4322): not the model provider, not a fetch. Two doors,
// one guard each, both here because both modes load this file. WP_Http: a request no test
// stubbed is refused, naming the URL, at the last priority, so a test's own
// `pre_http_request` stub (ToolkitsTest, ShortcodesTest) answers first and this sees only what
// nobody answered. The model provider: the configured Ollama provider talks through Symfony's
// HttpClient, which no `pre_http_request` sees, and ModelCatalog forgives a provider that
// fails, so a test that touched the catalog without TestCase::fakeProvider() made a real
// connection attempt and heard nothing. It is swapped for OfflineProvider at the first priority,
// so fakeProvider() (default priority) still replaces it; a WP AI client provider is left alone,
// since core routes it through WP_Http, which the first guard already covers.
tests_add_filter('pre_http_request', static function (mixed $pre, array $args, string $url): mixed {
    return $pre !== false
        ? $pre
        : new WP_Error('alpaca_bot_tests_offline', 'The integration suite makes no network requests; stub this one with pre_http_request: ' . $url);
}, PHP_INT_MAX, 3);
tests_add_filter(
    'alpaca_bot/provider',
    static fn(mixed $provider): mixed => $provider instanceof \AlpacaBot\Vendor\CarmeloSantana\PHPAgents\Provider\OllamaProvider
        ? new \AlpacaBot\Tests\Integration\OfflineProvider()
        : $provider,
    1,
);
```

These are stored values the tests round-trip, not calls. The guard means no host is ever dialled; `.invalid` means one that slipped could never resolve anyway.

  ```
  # WP_Http request a test did not stub and swaps the configured model provider for one that
  # throws, so a pass here is the same on a runner with no provider and no DNS (Kanboard #4322).
  ```

### Task 3: "Provider error:" only where the provider threw (Kanboard #4327, option a)

`Chat\Pipeline::send()` wraps everything its inner catch receives as `'Provider error: ' . $e->getMessage()` (`src/Chat/Pipeline.php:354`). The ticket's `:326` is that line before later edits. Two things reach it that the provider had no part in:

1. **A run whose Error finish nobody announced.** `failure()` (`:593-609`) returns the plugin's "The run ended in an error the assistant did not report." `send()` raises it (`:297`), and the catch prefixes it.
2. **A toolkit's `TerminationException` that escapes the agent's catch.** `AbstractAgent::run()` collects every toolkit's `tools()` at `vendor-prefixed/carmelosantana/php-agents/src/Agent/AbstractAgent.php:143`, before its first `try` (`:242`). A toolkit that stops the run from `tools()` escapes the fiber into `send()`'s catch as `Provider error: ` plus an empty message.

`Registry::enabled()` never calls `tools()` (`src/Toolkit/Registry.php:63-96`), so the first `tools()` call is inside the fiber.

The serial-path `TerminationException('')`, the one the ticket names, already finishes as a stop. The #4311 review dropped the `content !== ''` guard that made it a failure (Kanboard #4311 comment 609), and `tests/Unit/Chat/PipelineToolsTest.php:1079-1093` pins it. That test stays as it is.

Option (a):
- The prefix stays where the provider threw: the plain path's `stream()` and `factory->make()`, and a tool run whose failure the provider announced.
- Case 1 is raised in the plugin's words as they are. It gets a marker class, `Chat\RunFailure`, so `send()` can tell it apart without reading messages.
- Case 2 gets its own sentence when it has no words of its own, and its own message when it does.
- Everything else that lands in the catch keeps the prefix. That covers a consumer throwing into the generator (`PipelineToolsTest.php:251`) and the `LogicException` at `:1031`. The spec scopes option (a) to the two cases above; see the drafting notes.

**Strings** (text domain `alpaca-bot`, `alpaca-bot.php:11`). None has a placeholder, so none needs a `translators:` comment.
- Kept, unchanged: `'The run ended in an error the assistant did not report.'` Now raised without the prefix.
- New: `'The provider gave no reason.'` An announcement that was itself empty; the provider threw, so the prefix stays: `Provider error: The provider gave no reason.`
- New: `'A tool stopped the run without giving a reason.'` A `TerminationException('')` that escaped the agent; no prefix.
- `'Provider error: '` itself stays an untranslated literal. It is the established contract that `ChatCommandTest.php:298,331-333`, `ChatControllerTest.php:163`, `ErrorsTest.php:99` and the `docs/api.md:719` example all quote. Those cases are provider throws and don't change.

```php
/** Pipeline::failure(), which is private and static: the exception a failed run is raised as, or null. */
function pipelineFailureException(Output $output, AgentStreamObserver $observer): ?\RuntimeException
{
    /** @var ?\RuntimeException */
    return (new ReflectionMethod(Pipeline::class, 'failure'))->invoke(null, $output, $observer);
}

/** The message of that exception, or null: what the cases below pin. */
function pipelineFailure(Output $output, AgentStreamObserver $observer): ?string
{
    return pipelineFailureException($output, $observer)?->getMessage();
}

/** Pipeline::raised(), which is private and static: the message send() raises for what a failed turn threw. */
function pipelineRaised(\Throwable $e): string
{
    /** @var string */
    return (new ReflectionMethod(Pipeline::class, 'raised'))->invoke(null, $e);
}

/** The words an announcement with none borrows, as Pipeline::failure() writes them. */
const NO_REASON = 'The provider gave no reason.';

/** The words a tool that stopped the run with none gets, as Pipeline::raised() writes them. */
const TOOL_STOPPED = 'A tool stopped the run without giving a reason.';
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `src/Chat/RunFailure.php`:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace AlpacaBot\Chat;

/**
 * A tool turn that failed in this plugin's own words: its run ended on an Error finish nobody
 * announced and no successful tool result accounts for (Pipeline::failure() says how that is
 * read). Pipeline::send() raises its message as it is. The `Provider error:` prefix says the
 * provider threw, and nothing announced that it did (Kanboard #4327).
 *
 * A RuntimeException, so Errors::fromPipeline(), which maps a failed turn by class, maps it as it
 * mapped the RuntimeException this replaced.
 */
final class RunFailure extends \RuntimeException {}
```
In `src/Chat/Pipeline.php`:
- Add `use AlpacaBot\Vendor\CarmeloSantana\PHPAgents\Exception\TerminationException;` in sorted position.
- `send()` `:292-298` becomes:
```php
                    $failure = self::failure($output, $observer);
                    if ($failure !== null) {
                        // The run failed, and failure() has already said whose words the failure
                        // is in. Raising it here puts it on the path a plain provider failure takes
                        // (the catch below, which keeps the partial transcript when a tool had run).
                        throw $failure;
                    }
```
- The `finally` at `:348-355` becomes:
```php
                } finally {
                    // Thrown from the finally so a listener that throws cannot replace the
                    // failure with its own exception: a caller maps what arrives by class
                    // (ChatController reads an InvalidArgumentException as the user's mistake,
                    // 400). PHP chains a pending exception behind the one thrown here, so a
                    // listener's is kept, after $e. raised() says what the message is.
                    throw new \RuntimeException(self::raised($e), 0, $e);
                }
```
- In the `alpaca_bot/chat/failed` docblock (`:333-346`), replace the sentence "A listener that throws does not replace the provider error: that is rethrown as a RuntimeException with the listener's exception chained behind it." with:
  > A listener that throws does not replace the failure: that is rethrown as a RuntimeException, `Provider error: ` and what the provider threw, or in the plugin's own words where the provider had no part in it, with the listener's exception chained behind it.
- `@throws` at `:174`: "or wrapping a provider failure as 'Provider error: ...'" becomes:
  > or wrapping a provider failure as 'Provider error: ...' (a tool turn's failure the provider had no part in is raised in the plugin's own words instead: raised())
- `failure()`: the signature becomes `private static function failure(Output $output, AgentStreamObserver $observer): ?\RuntimeException`. The body:
```php
    {
        if ($output->finishReason !== AgentFinishReason::Error) {
            return null;
        }
        $announced = $observer->error();
        if ($announced === null) {
            foreach ($output->toolResults as $result) {
                if ($result->status === ToolResultStatus::Success && $result->content === $output->content) {
                    return null;
                }
            }
            return new RunFailure(__('The run ended in an error the assistant did not report.', 'alpaca-bot'));
        }
        return new \RuntimeException($announced !== '' ? $announced : __('The provider gave no reason.', 'alpaca-bot'));
    }
```
  The decision order is the one the docblock argues for: the announcement first, then the termination test, and only for an unannounced Error. In its docblock:
  - The first sentence, "The message a run failed on, or null when it did not fail.", becomes "The exception a run that failed is raised as, or null when it did not fail."
  - The last paragraph (`:586-591`, "The announcement supplies the words when it had any: …") is replaced with:
    > The announcement supplies the words when it had any, and the failure is the provider's: a plain RuntimeException, which send() raises as `Provider error: ` and those words (raised()). An announcement that is itself empty (the provider threw a Throwable whose getMessage() is '') is still the provider's failure and keeps the prefix; it has no words to lend, so it borrows this plugin's. Without an announcement there is nothing to quote and no provider failure to report: the content is the library's own prose, never announced. The failure is a RunFailure in words of this plugin's own, which translate, and which send() raises without the prefix (Kanboard #4327).
- New private static method, placed directly after `failure()`:
```php
    /**
     * The message send() raises a failed turn with: `Provider error: ` and what was thrown,
     * except for two failures the provider had no part in, which arrive in words of their own.
     *
     * A RunFailure is failure()'s, for a tool run whose Error finish nobody announced, and its
     * message is raised as it is. A TerminationException that reaches here escaped the agent's
     * own catch: AbstractAgent::run() collects every toolkit's tools() before its first try, so
     * a toolkit that stops the run from there is not caught by the library the way a tool that
     * stops it from execute() is (that one is a finished turn: failure()). It says what the tool
     * said, or that a tool stopped the run when it said nothing.
     *
     * Everything else keeps the prefix, as every failure did before 0.6: a provider that threw
     * on the plain path, one the agent announced, and whatever else lands in send()'s catch.
     */
    private static function raised(\Throwable $e): string
    {
        return match (true) {
            $e instanceof RunFailure => $e->getMessage(),
            $e instanceof TerminationException => $e->getMessage() !== '' ? $e->getMessage() : __('A tool stopped the run without giving a reason.', 'alpaca-bot'),
            default => 'Provider error: ' . $e->getMessage(),
        };
    }
```
- `agentTurn()`'s `:1037` arm, `self::failure($output, $observer) === null ? …`, reads the same with the new return type. Leave it.
- `docs/api.md` §5, the 502 row. Replace the row with:
  ```
  | 502 | `alpaca_bot_provider_error` | The model provider failed or could not be built; also a tool turn whose run failed without the provider saying so, or that a tool stopped before it began | `detail` (administrators only): `Provider error: …` and the provider's own text when the provider threw, or the plugin's own sentence when it had no part in it |
  ```

- `:1159` (the model resolver's outage path). A provider failure: true.
- `:986-990` in `agentTurn()`'s docblock ("A failure (the provider threw; …)"). Still true of what the Output carries.

### Task 4: Bound `Migrate04::migrateConversations()` with a per-row attempt count (Kanboard #4335)

**The loop it closes.** `migrateConversations()` (`src/Settings/Migrate04.php:270-309`) takes up to `BATCH` (100) `chat_history` rows still in `publish`. It flips each to `private` through `wp_update_post()`, and it sets `FLAG_CONVERSATIONS` only when a batch comes back short (`:306-308`). The rows are their own cursor. A row another plugin keeps in `publish` (`wp_insert_post_data` putting the status back, `wp_insert_post_empty_content` returning true) never leaves the result set.

With `BATCH` or more such rows, every batch is full and the flag is never written. That is 100 `wp_update_post()` calls on every request, anonymous front-end ones included: `Plugin::register()` hooks the migration on `init` priority 20 (`src/Plugin.php:161-166`). With fewer, the first short batch sets the flag and the pass ends; the rows are skipped already, just without saying so.

**Design.**
- **Where the count lives:** post meta `ab_migration_attempts` on the row (`Migrate04::META_ATTEMPTS`), named like `ConversationStore::META_MESSAGES = 'ab_messages'` (`src/Chat/ConversationStore.php:28`).
- **Counting.** After a batch's loop, one query asks which of the batch's ids are still `publish`, and each of those gets its count raised by one. On a site where every update takes, that query returns nothing and no meta is ever written.
- **The cap:** `MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3`. The batch query adds a `meta_query`: no count yet, or a count under the cap. A row at the cap leaves the result set the way a flipped row does.
- **What happens at the cap:** the row is left as it is (`publish`, carrying `ab_migration_attempts = 3`), and the pass goes on. When every remaining row is at the cap, the batch comes back short and the flag is written.

**Cost.** Everything below applies only while `FLAG_CONVERSATIONS` is unset. Once it is set, `conversationsPending()` is one alloptions read, as now (class docblock `:27-33`).
- The batch query gains a meta join.
- A non-empty batch costs one extra `SELECT … IN (ids)`.
- On a fresh install the batch is empty, so the extra query never runs. That keeps `Migrate04Test.php:66`'s "two `get_posts` calls" true.

**Limit, stated in the docblock.** A request that dies inside the loop counts nothing, because the count comes after it. The bound is for a write that fails, not one that takes the request down, which the site's error log already shows.

**Files:**
- Modify `src/Settings/Migrate04.php`: the constants (after `FLAG_AUTOLOAD`, `:51`), `migrateConversations()` `:258-309` (docblock, query, the count call, and the comment at `:294-305`), and a new private `countFailures()`.
- Test: `tests/Unit/Settings/Migrate04Test.php`, where two existing tests gain the new query and one test is new.
- Test: `tests/Integration/Migrate04ConversationsTest.php` (new).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces: `Migrate04::META_ATTEMPTS = 'ab_migration_attempts'`, `Migrate04::MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3` (both `public const`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/Integration/Migrate04ConversationsTest.php`. This is the real proof: real rows, a real filter keeping them in `publish`, and four requests.
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace AlpacaBot\Tests\Integration;

use AlpacaBot\Chat\ConversationStore;
use AlpacaBot\Settings\Migrate04;
use AlpacaBot\Settings\Store;

/**
 * The conversation pass against a real posts table, with another plugin's filter keeping every
 * legacy row in `publish` (Kanboard #4335). The unit suite can only assert the query it hands
 * get_posts(); what the meta query leaves out, and whether the pass ends, is the database's to say.
 *
 * @group migration
 */
final class Migrate04ConversationsTest extends TestCase
{
    /** Every step but the conversation pass is done, so run() is that pass and nothing else. */
    private function onlyConversationsPending(): void
    {
        foreach ([Migrate04::FLAG, Migrate04::FLAG_RETENTION, Migrate04::FLAG_AUTOLOAD] as $flag) {
            update_option($flag, '1', true);
        }
        delete_option(Migrate04::FLAG_CONVERSATIONS);
    }

    public function test_a_full_batch_another_plugin_keeps_in_publish_is_tried_max_attempts_times_and_then_left(): void
    {
        $this->onlyConversationsPending();
        $owner = self::factory()->user->create();
        // A full batch. Fewer rows than BATCH end the pass on the first request whatever became of
        // them, which is not the case the bound is for.
        $ids = self::factory()->post->create_many(100, ['post_type' => ConversationStore::POST_TYPE, 'post_status' => 'publish', 'post_author' => $owner]);
        $tries = 0;
        add_filter('wp_insert_post_data', static function (array $data, array $postarr) use (&$tries): array {
            if (($data['post_type'] ?? '') === ConversationStore::POST_TYPE && !empty($postarr['ID'])) {
                ++$tries;
                $data['post_status'] = 'publish';
            }
            return $data;
        }, 10, 2);

        for ($request = 1; $request <= Migrate04::MAX_ATTEMPTS; $request++) {
            $migration = new Migrate04(new Store());
            $this->assertTrue($migration->needed(), "request {$request} still has work");
            $migration->run();
            $this->assertSame(100 * $request, $tries, "request {$request} tried every row once");
            $this->assertFalse(get_option(Migrate04::FLAG_CONVERSATIONS), 'a full batch leaves the pass open');
        }
        foreach ($ids as $id) {
            $this->assertSame('publish', get_post_status($id));
            $this->assertSame((string) Migrate04::MAX_ATTEMPTS, get_post_meta($id, Migrate04::META_ATTEMPTS, true));
        }

        // The next request asks for none of them, writes none, and ends the pass.
        (new Migrate04(new Store()))->run();
        $this->assertSame(100 * Migrate04::MAX_ATTEMPTS, $tries);
        $this->assertSame('1', get_option(Migrate04::FLAG_CONVERSATIONS));
        $this->assertFalse((new Migrate04(new Store()))->needed());
    }

    public function test_rows_that_move_carry_no_attempt_count(): void
    {
        $this->onlyConversationsPending();
        $owner = self::factory()->user->create();
        $ids = self::factory()->post->create_many(3, ['post_type' => ConversationStore::POST_TYPE, 'post_status' => 'publish', 'post_author' => $owner]);

        (new Migrate04(new Store()))->run();

        foreach ($ids as $id) {
            $this->assertSame('private', get_post_status($id));
            $this->assertSame('', get_post_meta($id, Migrate04::META_ATTEMPTS, true));
        }
        $this->assertSame('1', get_option(Migrate04::FLAG_CONVERSATIONS));
    }
}
```

```php
// Kanboard #4335: a row another plugin keeps in `publish` is counted after each batch it

it('counts a failed try on every row a batch left in publish, and asks only for rows under the cap', function (): void {
    $stored = [Migrate04::FLAG => '1', Migrate04::FLAG_RETENTION => '1', Migrate04::FLAG_AUTOLOAD => '1'];

        ->and($meta)->not->toHaveKey(12)
        // Three rows is a short batch: the pass is done whatever became of them.
        ->and($stored[Migrate04::FLAG_CONVERSATIONS])->toBe('1');
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `src/Settings/Migrate04.php`, after `FLAG_AUTOLOAD` (`:51`):
```php
    /** Post meta on a legacy conversation row: how many batches have tried to move it out of `publish` and found it still there. */
    public const META_ATTEMPTS = 'ab_migration_attempts';

    /**
     * Tries per row before the conversation pass stops asking for it. Three rather than one
     * because a failure can be passing (a lock, a filter that only objects mid-request); a few
     * rather than many because every try is one wp_update_post() and its hook chain, paid by
     * whichever request comes next, a visitor's page view included.
     */
    public const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3;
```
Replace `migrateConversations()` and its docblock (`:258-309`) with:
```php
    /**
     * One batch of 0.4 conversation rows.
     *
     * 0.4 stored every conversation as a `publish` post and left post_author to
     * wp_insert_post()'s default — the current user, so 0 for a request that had none.
     * ConversationStore checks the owner strictly, so every legacy row becomes private, and one
     * with no owner takes it from the first message: 0.4 wrote the user's id as that message's
     * role. A row that already has an owner keeps it.
     *
     * Only `publish` rows under MAX_ATTEMPTS failed tries are queried, so a row leaves the next
     * batch's result set by being flipped or by reaching the cap: the rows are their own cursor,
     * and the pass is complete when a batch comes back short.
     *
     * The cap is what makes the pass end. This plugin's own write always takes: the post type
     * supports title, excerpt and author but not `editor` (ConversationStore::registerPostType()),
     * and wp_insert_post()'s empty-content refusal needs all three of those supports
     * (WP 7.1 post.php:4673-4677), so it cannot fire on these rows. Another plugin can still keep
     * a row in `publish` (`wp_insert_post_data` putting the status back,
     * `wp_insert_post_empty_content` returning true), or an UPDATE can keep failing. Without the
     * cap, a full batch of such rows came back on every request, forever, front-end page views
     * included (Kanboard #4335). With it, each is tried MAX_ATTEMPTS times (countFailures()) and
     * then left as it is: still `publish`, carrying its count under META_ATTEMPTS, where anyone
     * investigating finds it. A request that dies inside the loop counts nothing, since the count
     * comes after it. The bound is for a write that fails, not for one that takes the request
     * down, which the site's error log already shows.
     */
    private function migrateConversations(): void
    {
        $posts = get_posts([
            'post_type' => 'chat_history',
            'post_status' => 'publish',
            'numberposts' => self::BATCH,
            'orderby' => 'ID',
            'order' => 'ASC',
            'meta_query' => [
                'relation' => 'OR',
                ['key' => self::META_ATTEMPTS, 'compare' => 'NOT EXISTS'],
                ['key' => self::META_ATTEMPTS, 'value' => self::MAX_ATTEMPTS, 'compare' => '<', 'type' => 'NUMERIC'],
            ],
        ]);
        foreach ($posts as $post) {
            $pid = (int) $post->ID;
            $update = ['ID' => $pid, 'post_status' => 'private'];
            if ((int) $post->post_author < 1) {
                $legacy = get_post_meta($pid, 'messages', true);
                $role = is_array($legacy) ? ($legacy[0]['message']['role'] ?? null) : null;
                if (is_int($role) && $role > 0) {
                    $update['post_author'] = $role;
                }
            }
            // wp_update_post() unslashes the fields it is given. These are an id, a status and
            // sometimes an author id, none of which can carry a backslash, so this changes
            // nothing today; it is here so the rule holds at every write, whatever is added.
            wp_update_post(wp_slash($update));
        }
        if ($posts !== []) {
            $this->countFailures(array_map(static fn(object $p): int => (int) $p->ID, $posts));
        }
        if (count($posts) < self::BATCH) {
            update_option(self::FLAG_CONVERSATIONS, '1', true);
        }
    }

    /**
     * One more failed try on every row of the batch still in `publish` after its update: one
     * query for the batch, and a meta write only for a row that did not move. On a site where
     * every update takes, which is every site this plugin alone runs on, it writes nothing. The
     * batch query already primed these rows' meta, so reading the count costs no query.
     *
     * @param list<int> $ids the batch, in the order it was read
     */
    private function countFailures(array $ids): void
    {
        $stuck = get_posts([
            'post_type' => 'chat_history',
            'post_status' => 'publish',
            'post__in' => $ids,
            'numberposts' => count($ids),
            'fields' => 'ids',
            'no_found_rows' => true,
            'update_post_meta_cache' => false,
            'update_post_term_cache' => false,
        ]);
        foreach ($stuck as $id) {
            $id = (int) $id;
            // wp_slash() for the rule the loop above states: every write that unslashes is handed a slashed value.
            update_post_meta($id, self::META_ATTEMPTS, wp_slash((int) get_post_meta($id, self::META_ATTEMPTS, true) + 1));
        }
    }
```

### Task 5: Extract `Chat\FakedToolCall` from `Pipeline` (pure refactor)

**The logic being moved.** These are the private statics of `src/Chat/Pipeline.php`:

| Pipeline (private) | Lines (docblock through body) | `FakedToolCall` | Visibility |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `recovered(string $content): string` | `:611-704` | `recovered()` | public |
| `markup(string $content): array` | `:706-781` | `markup()` | public |
| `fakedPieces(string $text, int $offset): array` | `:783-802` | `pieces()` | private |
| `unfenced(string $block): ?array` | `:804-814` | `unfenced()` | private |
| `excisions(string $content, array $found): ?array` | `:816-857` | `excisions()` | public |
| `answer(array $calls): ?string` | `:859-884` | `answer()` | public |
| `fakedCalls(string $block): ?array` | `:886-905` | `calls()` | public |

There is one call site, `$content = self::recovered($content);` at `:291`. There are no other references in `src/`, `tests/` or `docs/` (`git grep` for each name: the only other hits are `Shortcodes\Chat::answer()`, a different method).

**The P5 fuzz.** Kanboard #2978 comment 632 records that "the faked-tool-call recovery survived 21,957 fuzzed inputs with zero defects" in the P5 deep code review. The harness itself is gone:
- nothing in `tests/` or `bin/` (`grep -rni fuzz` matches only the spec);
- nothing in git history (`git log --all -i --grep=fuzz` matches nothing);
- the P5 ledger was gitignored in a worktree that was removed at close (#2978 comment 638).

This task rebuilds the fuzz as a seeded *differential* run of the same size: 21,957 generated inputs through `Pipeline::recovered()` before the move, and through `FakedToolCall::recovered()` after it, every output hash compared. That is the right check for a pure refactor, and it adds the two never-lose-text invariants the docblock states. The script stays in scratch, not in the repo: after this task there is no "before" to diff against.

- Create: `tests/Unit/Chat/FakedToolCallTest.php`
- Modify `src/Chat/Pipeline.php`:

  - the class docblock at `:83` and the comment at `:283-290`, as below.
- Test: `tests/Unit/Chat/FakedToolCallTest.php`; `tests/Unit/Chat/PipelineToolsTest.php` stays unchanged and green.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing. Task 3 has changed `failure()` in the same file; this task does not touch it.
- Produces, for Task 16 (`final class AlpacaBot\Chat\FakedToolCall`, `src/Chat/FakedToolCall.php`):
  - `public const OPEN = '<tool_call>';` and `public const CLOSE = '</tool_call>';`
  - `public static function recovered(string $content): string`: the reply with faked calls taken out and a faked `done`'s answer put back; `$content` byte for byte on each of its three documented ways out.
  - `public static function markup(string $content): array`, returning `list<array{0: int, 1: int, 2: list<ToolCall>|null}>`: every candidate byte range, null for a marker.
  - `public static function excisions(string $content, array $found): ?array`, returning `list<array{0: int, 1: int, 2: string}>|null`: the ranges that are markup, with what replaces each. Null when a `done` carried no answer.
  - `public static function answer(array $calls): ?string`: what a block of faked calls leaves behind.
  - `public static function calls(string $block): ?array`, returning `non-empty-list<ToolCall>|null`: the vendored parser's reading, null for prose.
  - The `ToolCall` above is `AlpacaBot\Vendor\CarmeloSantana\PHPAgents\Tool\ToolCall`.
  - Task 16 owns this sentence, moved verbatim into `recovered()`'s docblock: "The deltas already streamed still carry the markup; suppressing it live is separate." Task 16 falsifies it and must rewrite it.

- [ ] **Step 0: Capture the differential baseline, before touching `src/`**

Write this script to your scratchpad (not the repo), for example `"$SCRATCH/faked-fuzz.php"`:
````php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

// Differential fuzz for the Chat\FakedToolCall extraction (0.6 plan, Task 5). Not committed.
//   php faked-fuzz.php <repo> capture <file>   before the move: Pipeline::recovered(), by reflection
//   php faked-fuzz.php <repo> compare <file>   after it: FakedToolCall::recovered()
// 21,957 inputs, the size of the P5 deep review's run, from a fixed seed, so both runs see the same ones.

[, $repo, $mode, $file] = $argv + [null, null, null, null];
if (!is_string($repo) || !in_array($mode, ['capture', 'compare'], true) || !is_string($file)) {
    fwrite(STDERR, "usage: php faked-fuzz.php <repo> capture|compare <file>\n");
    exit(2);
}
require $repo . '/vendor/autoload.php';
require $repo . '/vendor-prefixed/autoload.php';

$recover = $mode === 'capture'
    ? static fn(string $s): string => (new ReflectionMethod('AlpacaBot\Chat\Pipeline', 'recovered'))->invoke(null, $s)
    : static fn(string $s): string => AlpacaBot\Chat\FakedToolCall::recovered($s);

$done = static fn(string $r): string => '{"name": "done", "arguments": {"response": ' . json_encode($r) . '}}';
$pool = [
    'Let me answer that.', 'Here is the fix:', 'That is all.', 'Some prose that says <tool_call> in passing.',
    '<tool_call>', '</tool_call>', '<tool_call>', '</tool_call>',
    $done('An alpaca is a camelid.'), $done(''), $done('   '), $done("    if (\$x) {\n        run();\n    }  "),
    '{"name": "done", "arguments": {}}', '{"name": "done", "arguments": {"response": 42}}',
    '{"name": "web_fetch", "arguments": {"url": "https://example.com"}}', '{"name": "draft_post", "arguments": {"text": "Hello"}}',
    '{"tool_calls": []}', '{"name": ', 'not json {', '{}',
    "```json\n" . $done('fenced') . "\n```", "```\n" . $done('bare fence') . "\n```",
    "    if (\$x) {\n\n        run();\n    }", 'C:\Users\x and a regex \d+',
    'Write <tool_call>' . $done('x') . '</tool_call> to fake a call.',
    (string) file_get_contents($repo . '/tests/fixtures/faked-tool-call-qwen3-vl-2b.txt'),
];
$glue = ['', "\n", "\n\n", "\n   \n", "\r\n\r\n", ' ', "\n\n\n", "\t\n"];

mt_srand(20260915);
$hashes = [];
$changed = 0;
$violations = [];
for ($i = 0; $i < 21957; $i++) {
    $in = mt_rand(0, 3) === 0 ? $glue[mt_rand(0, count($glue) - 1)] : '';
    for ($k = mt_rand(1, 6); $k > 0; $k--) {
        $in .= $pool[mt_rand(0, count($pool) - 1)] . $glue[mt_rand(0, count($glue) - 1)];
    }
    $out = $recover($in);
    $hashes[] = hash('sha256', $out);
    $changed += $out === $in ? 0 : 1;
    // The two never-lose-text rules recovered()'s docblock states: nothing comes back empty,
    // and content with no marker at all comes back byte for byte.
    if ($in !== '' && $out === '') {
        $violations[] = "#{$i} empty output";
    }
    if (!str_contains($in, '<tool_call>') && !str_contains($in, '</tool_call>') && $out !== $in) {
        $violations[] = "#{$i} markerless input rewritten";
    }
}
$mismatches = [];
if ($mode === 'capture') {
    file_put_contents($file, json_encode($hashes));
} else {
    $before = json_decode((string) file_get_contents($file), true);
    foreach ($hashes as $i => $h) {
        if (($before[$i] ?? null) !== $h) {
            $mismatches[] = $i;
        }
    }
}
printf("%s inputs=%d changed=%d invariant_violations=%d mismatches=%d\n", $mode, count($hashes), $changed, count($violations), count($mismatches));
foreach (array_slice([...$violations, ...array_map(static fn(int $i): string => "#{$i} differs from before", $mismatches)], 0, 5) as $line) {
    echo "  {$line}\n";
}
exit($violations === [] && $mismatches === [] ? 0 : 1);
````

Expected: `capture inputs=21957 changed=<N> invariant_violations=0 mismatches=0`, exit 0. Record `N`; it must be well above 0, or the generator is not reaching the markup paths. If a violation appears here, it is a pre-existing defect, not this task's. Stop and report it with its input.

````php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use AlpacaBot\Chat\FakedToolCall;
use AlpacaBot\Vendor\CarmeloSantana\PHPAgents\Tool\ToolCall;

/*
 * Chat\FakedToolCall on its own: the recovery Pipeline runs on a finished tool turn, driven
 * directly. Every case PipelineToolsTest drives through a whole agent run (:632-973) is here as
 * one input and one expected reply, so a change to the recovery costs a string, not a harness.
 * PipelineToolsTest keeps the cases that are about the run: that a leaked call is never
 * executed, never recorded in meta['tool_calls'], and that what is stored is what came back.
 */

function fakedDone(string $response = 'An alpaca is a camelid.'): string
{
    return '{"name": "done", "arguments": {"response": ' . json_encode($response) . '}}';
}

dataset('faked tool calls', function (): array {
    $done = fakedDone();
    $fetch = '{"name": "web_fetch", "arguments": {"url": "https://example.com"}}';
    $code = "Here is the fix:\n\n    if (\$x) {\n\n        run();\n    }\n\nThat is all.";
    $block = "    if (\$x) {\n        run();\n    }";
    $indent = '{"name": "done", "arguments": {"response": "Indent it by four spaces."}}';
    $fence = "```json\n{$done}\n```";

    return [
        'no marker: untouched' => ["Plain answer.\n\n    code\n", "Plain answer.\n\n    code\n"],
        'a well-formed done, markers and all' => ["Let me answer that.\n\n<tool_call>\n{$done}\n</tool_call>", "Let me answer that.\n\nAn alpaca is a camelid."],
        'a call that is not done is dropped' => ["I will draft that for you.\n\n<tool_call>{\"name\": \"draft_post\", \"arguments\": {\"text\": \"Hello\"}}</tool_call>\n\nDone.", "I will draft that for you.\n\nDone."],
        'prose that mentions the marker' => [
            "A model without a tools template writes <tool_call> markers into its answer.\n\nThey look like this: <tool_call>...</tool_call>, and they are not calls at all.",
            "A model without a tools template writes <tool_call> markers into its answer.\n\nThey look like this: <tool_call>...</tool_call>, and they are not calls at all.",
        ],
        'a blank done: the recovery is off' => ['<tool_call>{"name": "done", "arguments": {"response": ""}}</tool_call>', '<tool_call>{"name": "done", "arguments": {"response": ""}}</tool_call>'],
        'a done with no response: the recovery is off' => ['<tool_call>{"name": "done", "arguments": {}}</tool_call>', '<tool_call>{"name": "done", "arguments": {}}</tool_call>'],
        'a recovery that leaves nothing gives the raw JSON back' => ['<tool_call>{"name": "draft_post", "arguments": {"text": "Hello"}}</tool_call>', '<tool_call>{"name": "draft_post", "arguments": {"text": "Hello"}}</tool_call>'],
        'a blank done after prose keeps prose and markup' => ["Hello there.\n\n<tool_call>{\"name\": \"done\", \"arguments\": {\"response\": \"\"}}</tool_call>", "Hello there.\n\n<tool_call>{\"name\": \"done\", \"arguments\": {\"response\": \"\"}}</tool_call>"],
        'an indented code block before a marked done' => ["{$code}\n\n<tool_call>{$indent}</tool_call>", "{$code}\n\nIndent it by four spaces."],
        'a marker-less call beside an indented code block' => ["<tool_call>{$fetch}</tool_call>\n\n{$code}\n\n{$indent}", "{$code}\n\nIndent it by four spaces."],
        'a stray closing marker on the next line' => ["Let me check.\n\n{$done}\n</tool_call>", "Let me check.\n\nAn alpaca is a camelid."],
        'a stray closing marker on the call\'s line' => ["Let me check.\n\n{$done}</tool_call>", "Let me check.\n\nAn alpaca is a camelid."],
        'both marker-less calls of the recorded shape' => ["Let me check.\n\n{$fetch}\n\n{$done}\n</tool_call>", "Let me check.\n\nAn alpaca is a camelid."],
        'a blank line with spaces on it' => ["Let me check.\n   \n{$done}\n</tool_call>", "Let me check.\n   \nAn alpaca is a camelid."],
        'an opening marker with prose before the call' => ["<tool_call>\n\nSome prose.\n\n{$done}", "<tool_call>\n\nSome prose.\n\nAn alpaca is a camelid."],
        'a closing marker with prose after the call' => ["{$done}\n\nSome prose.\n\n</tool_call>", "An alpaca is a camelid.\n\nSome prose.\n\n</tool_call>"],
        'no orphan marker where a recovered call sat' => ["<tool_call>\n\n{$done}\n\nHere is more prose.", "An alpaca is a camelid.\n\nHere is more prose."],
        'an answer about tool calls, fenced example and all' => [
            "Tool calls look like <tool_call>.\n\nExample:\n\n```json\n{$fetch}\n```\n\nThat's it.",
            "Tool calls look like <tool_call>.\n\nExample:\n\n```json\n{$fetch}\n```\n\nThat's it.",
        ],
        'a fenced example that is the whole head' => ["{$fence}\n\n<tool_call>", "{$fence}\n\n<tool_call>"],
        'a fenced example that is the whole tail' => ["</tool_call>\n\n{$fence}", "</tool_call>\n\n{$fence}"],
        'a fenced call between the markers' => ["<tool_call>\n{$fence}\n</tool_call>", 'An alpaca is a camelid.'],
        'a call written inside a line of prose' => ['Write <tool_call>' . fakedDone('x') . '</tool_call> to fake a call.', 'Write <tool_call>' . fakedDone('x') . '</tool_call> to fake a call.'],
        'a done answer that is itself indented' => ["Here:\n\n<tool_call>{\"name\": \"done\", \"arguments\": {\"response\": \"    if (\$x) {\\n        run();\\n    }  \"}}</tool_call>", "Here:\n\n{$block}"],
        'the first line\'s indentation after a removal at the head' => ["<tool_call>{$fetch}</tool_call>\n\n{$block}\n\nThat is all.", "{$block}\n\nThat is all."],
        'the first line\'s indentation after a removal in the middle' => ["Here:\n\n<tool_call>{$fetch}</tool_call>\n\n{$block}\n\n{$indent}", "Here:\n\n{$block}\n\nIndent it by four spaces."],
        'a payload that names no call' => ["Hello.\n\n<tool_call>{\"tool_calls\": []}</tool_call>", "Hello.\n\n<tool_call>{\"tool_calls\": []}</tool_call>"],
        'no blank line left where the markup was the whole reply' => ["\n\n<tool_call>{$done}</tool_call>\n\n", 'An alpaca is a camelid.'],
    ];
});

it('recovers what a faked call buried and keeps every other byte', function (string $content, string $expected): void {
    expect(FakedToolCall::recovered($content))->toBe($expected);
})->with('faked tool calls');

it('recovers the answer from the recorded qwen3-vl:2b leak', function (): void {
    $leak = (string) file_get_contents(__DIR__ . '/../../fixtures/faked-tool-call-qwen3-vl-2b.txt');

    expect(FakedToolCall::recovered($leak))
        ->toBe('An alpaca (Lama pacos) is a domesticated species of South American camelid, traditionally bred for their valuable fiber in the textile industry.');
});

it('names a marked call and both its markers as three touching byte ranges', function (): void {
    $done = fakedDone();
    $content = FakedToolCall::OPEN . $done . FakedToolCall::CLOSE;
    $open = strlen(FakedToolCall::OPEN);
    $end = $open + strlen($done);

    $found = FakedToolCall::markup($content);

    expect(array_map(static fn(array $r): array => [$r[0], $r[1], $r[2] === null], $found))
        ->toBe([[0, $open, true], [$open, $end, false], [$end, $end + strlen(FakedToolCall::CLOSE), true]])
        ->and($found[1][2][0]->name)->toBe('done')
        ->and(FakedToolCall::excisions($content, $found))
        ->toBe([[0, $open, ''], [$open, $end, 'An alpaca is a camelid.'], [$end, $end + strlen(FakedToolCall::CLOSE), '']]);
});

it('refuses a run that does not stand on its own lines, and calls the whole recovery off for a blank done', function (): void {
    $inline = 'Write ' . FakedToolCall::OPEN . fakedDone('x') . FakedToolCall::CLOSE . ' to fake a call.';
    $blank = FakedToolCall::OPEN . fakedDone('') . FakedToolCall::CLOSE;

    expect(FakedToolCall::excisions($inline, FakedToolCall::markup($inline)))->toBe([])
        ->and(FakedToolCall::excisions($blank, FakedToolCall::markup($blank)))->toBeNull();
});

it('answers with every done in a block, trailing whitespace off and leading kept, and refuses a done with nothing to say', function (): void {
    $block = [new ToolCall('a', 'done', ['response' => "  First.  \n"]), new ToolCall('b', 'web_fetch', ['url' => 'x']), new ToolCall('c', 'done', ['response' => 'Second.'])];

    expect(FakedToolCall::answer($block))->toBe("  First.\n\nSecond.")
        ->and(FakedToolCall::answer([new ToolCall('b', 'web_fetch', ['url' => 'x'])]))->toBe('')
        ->and(FakedToolCall::answer([new ToolCall('a', 'done', ['response' => '   '])]))->toBeNull()
        ->and(FakedToolCall::answer([new ToolCall('a', 'done', [])]))->toBeNull()
        ->and(FakedToolCall::answer([new ToolCall('a', 'done', ['response' => 42])]))->toBeNull();
});

it('reads a call payload as its calls and anything else, a payload holding none included, as prose', function (): void {
    $calls = FakedToolCall::calls(fakedDone('x'));

    expect($calls)->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and($calls[0]->name)->toBe('done')
        ->and($calls[0]->arguments)->toBe(['response' => 'x'])
        ->and(FakedToolCall::calls(''))->toBeNull()
        ->and(FakedToolCall::calls('Just prose.'))->toBeNull()
        ->and(FakedToolCall::calls('{"tool_calls": []}'))->toBeNull();
});
````

Expected: FAIL. Every case errors with `Class "AlpacaBot\Chat\FakedToolCall" not found`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `src/Chat/FakedToolCall.php`. The bodies are Pipeline's, byte for byte, except:
- `self::fakedCalls` becomes `self::calls`;
- `self::fakedPieces` becomes `self::pieces`;
- `recovered()`'s trigger reads the two constants.

Each method's docblock moves verbatim from the Pipeline line range in the table above, with only these edits:
- `fakedPieces`'s docblock stays as it is on `pieces()`;
- in `unfenced()`'s and `markup()`'s docblocks, a mention of "fakedCalls" becomes "calls()". `grep -n fakedCalls src/Chat/FakedToolCall.php` must come back empty.
````php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace AlpacaBot\Chat;

use AlpacaBot\Vendor\CarmeloSantana\PHPAgents\Provider\LlamaCpp\LlamaCppToolCallParser;
use AlpacaBot\Vendor\CarmeloSantana\PHPAgents\Tool\DoneTool;
use AlpacaBot\Vendor\CarmeloSantana\PHPAgents\Tool\ToolCall;

/**
 * A faked tool call: what a model whose deployed template cannot really call tools writes into
 * its reply instead of calling one, and the reply with it taken back out. recovered() is the
 * entry point, and its docblock is the argument; markup(), excisions(), answer() and calls() are
 * the steps it takes, public so each can be tested on its own and so a stream can use the same
 * knowledge a finished reply does. Every method is a function of its arguments: no state, and no
 * WordPress.
 *
 * Extracted from Chat\Pipeline's private statics in 0.6 with no change in behaviour.
 *
 * @since 0.6.0
 */
final class FakedToolCall
{
    /** The marker a template emits before a call. */
    public const OPEN = '<tool_call>';

    /** The marker a template emits after one. */
    public const CLOSE = '</tool_call>';

    /**
     * (moved verbatim from Pipeline.php:611-669)
     */
    public static function recovered(string $content): string
    {
        if (!str_contains($content, self::OPEN) && !str_contains($content, self::CLOSE)) {
            return $content;
        }
        $edits = self::excisions($content, self::markup($content));
        if ($edits === null || $edits === []) {
            return $content;
        }
        $reply = '';
        $cursor = 0;
        foreach ($edits as [$start, $end, $answer]) {
            if ($start > $cursor) {
                $reply .= substr($content, $cursor, $start - $cursor);
            }
            $cursor = max($cursor, $end);
            if ($answer !== '') {
                $reply .= $answer;
                continue;
            }
            // The blank line before the hole belongs to the text that is staying; the blank
            // lines after it went with the markup, to the first line with anything on it, whose
            // own indentation is not whitespace this may write and is left alone. Only where a
            // line had already ended, though — never after an answer just written in the
            // markup's place, and never inside a line of prose.
            if (($reply === '' || preg_match('~\R[^\S\r\n]*\z~', $reply) === 1)
                && preg_match('~\A[^\S\r\n]*\R(?:[^\S\r\n]*\R)*~', substr($content, $cursor), $seam) === 1
            ) {
                $cursor += strlen($seam[0]);
            }
        }
        $reply .= substr($content, $cursor);
        $reply = rtrim((string) preg_replace('~\A(?:[^\S\r\n]*\R)+~', '', $reply));
        return $reply === '' ? $content : $reply;
    }

    /**
     * (moved verbatim from Pipeline.php:706-740)
     *
     * @return list<array{0: int, 1: int, 2: list<ToolCall>|null}>
     */
    public static function markup(string $content): array
    {
        // phpcs:ignore Universal.Operators.DisallowShortTernary.Found -- The idiomatic guard for preg_split()'s false return; ?: reads better here than repeating the whole call in a full ternary.
        $segments = preg_split('~</?tool_call>~', $content, -1, PREG_SPLIT_OFFSET_CAPTURE) ?: [];
        $last = count($segments) - 1;
        $calls = [];
        $pieces = [];
        $against = [];
        foreach ($segments as $i => [$text, $offset]) {
            $end = $offset + strlen($text);
            $whole = trim($text);
            // Between two markers a fence is the call's own formatting; head and tail are
            // outside them, where a fence is an example the answer is entitled to keep.
            $calls[$i] = $i > 0 && $i < $last ? self::calls($whole) : self::unfenced($whole);
            $pieces[$i] = $calls[$i] === null ? self::pieces($text, $offset) : [];
            $first = $pieces[$i][0] ?? null;
            $final = $pieces[$i] === [] ? null : $pieces[$i][count($pieces[$i]) - 1];
            $against[$i] = [
                $calls[$i] !== null || ($first !== null && trim(substr($content, $offset, $first[0] - $offset)) === ''),
                $calls[$i] !== null || ($final !== null && trim(substr($content, $final[1], $end - $final[1])) === ''),
            ];
        }
        $found = [];
        $after = 0;
        foreach ($segments as $i => [$text, $offset]) {
            if ($i > 0 && ($against[$i - 1][1] || $against[$i][0])) {
                $found[] = [$after, $offset, null];
            }
            $after = $offset + strlen($text);
            $whole = $calls[$i];
            if ($whole !== null) {
                $found[] = [$offset, $after, $whole];
                continue;
            }
            foreach ($pieces[$i] as $piece) {
                $found[] = $piece;
            }
        }
        return $found;
    }

    /**
     * (moved verbatim from Pipeline.php:816-830)
     *
     * @param list<array{0: int, 1: int, 2: list<ToolCall>|null}> $found
     * @return list<array{0: int, 1: int, 2: string}>|null
     */
    public static function excisions(string $content, array $found): ?array
    {
        $edits = [];
        $count = count($found);
        $end = 0;
        for ($i = 0; $i < $count; $i = $end) {
            // One run: a call and the markers around it touch, byte to byte.
            $end = $i + 1;
            while ($end < $count && $found[$end][0] === $found[$end - 1][1]) {
                ++$end;
            }
            if (preg_match('~(?:\A|\R)[^\S\r\n]*\z~', substr($content, 0, $found[$i][0])) !== 1
                || preg_match('~\A[^\S\r\n]*(?:\R|\z)~', substr($content, $found[$end - 1][1])) !== 1
            ) {
                continue;
            }
            for ($at = $i; $at < $end; $at++) {
                [$start, $stop, $calls] = $found[$at];
                $answer = $calls === null ? '' : self::answer($calls);
                if ($answer === null) {
                    return null;
                }
                $edits[] = [$start, $stop, $answer];
            }
        }
        return $edits;
    }

    /**
     * (moved verbatim from Pipeline.php:859-869)
     *
     * @param list<ToolCall> $calls
     */
    public static function answer(array $calls): ?string
    {
        $answers = [];
        foreach ($calls as $call) {
            if ($call->name !== DoneTool::NAME) {
                continue;
            }
            $response = $call->arguments['response'] ?? null;
            if (!is_string($response) || trim($response) === '') {
                return null;
            }
            $answers[] = rtrim($response);
        }
        return implode("\n\n", $answers);
    }

    /**
     * (moved verbatim from Pipeline.php:886-896)
     *
     * @return non-empty-list<ToolCall>|null
     */
    public static function calls(string $block): ?array
    {
        try {
            $calls = array_values((new LlamaCppToolCallParser())->parse($block, 'json'));
        } catch (\Throwable) {
            return null;
        }
        return $calls === [] ? null : $calls;
    }

    /**
     * (moved verbatim from Pipeline.php:783-790)
     *
     * @return list<array{0: int, 1: int, 2: list<ToolCall>}>
     */
    private static function pieces(string $text, int $offset): array
    {
        $found = [];
        // phpcs:ignore Universal.Operators.DisallowShortTernary.Found -- The idiomatic guard for preg_split()'s false return; ?: reads better here than repeating the whole call in a full ternary.
        foreach (preg_split('~\R[ \t]*\R~', $text, -1, PREG_SPLIT_OFFSET_CAPTURE) ?: [] as [$piece, $at]) {
            $inside = self::unfenced(trim($piece));
            if ($inside !== null) {
                $found[] = [$offset + $at, $offset + $at + strlen($piece), $inside];
            }
        }
        return $found;
    }

    /**
     * (moved verbatim from Pipeline.php:804-810)
     *
     * @return non-empty-list<ToolCall>|null
     */
    private static function unfenced(string $block): ?array
    {
        return preg_match('~^```(?:json)?\s*.*?\s*```$~si', $block) === 1 ? null : self::calls($block);
    }
}
````
In `src/Chat/Pipeline.php`:

- `:291`: `$content = self::recovered($content);` becomes `$content = FakedToolCall::recovered($content);`. The class is in the same namespace, so no `use` is needed.
- Delete the `use` lines for `LlamaCppToolCallParser` (`:26`), `DoneTool` (`:27`) and `ToolCall` (`:28`). Check with `grep -nE 'LlamaCppToolCallParser|DoneTool|\bToolCall\b' src/Chat/Pipeline.php`, which must be empty afterwards.
- Class docblock `:83`: "(recovered(), which says how, and why the provider's capability data cannot be trusted to tell such a model apart)" becomes "(Chat\FakedToolCall::recovered(), which says how, and why the provider's capability data cannot be trusted to tell such a model apart)".
- The comment at `:283-290`: "(recovered(), which says why this cannot be left to the provider's capability data)" becomes "(FakedToolCall::recovered(), which says why this cannot be left to the provider's capability data)". The rest of that comment, about an abandoned turn storing the raw leak, stays true.

### Drafting notes (Phase A)

1. **Task 2: the spec's premise is mostly already met.**
   - `d46a096` (on `develop`, 2026-09-09) removed six `[#2977]` probes, not two.
   - It proved the `ollama.internal` line was fixture text logged by `Errors::provider()`, not DNS.
   - Kanboard #4322 was never closed.

   The one real gap is silent. `Factory` builds `OllamaProvider` on Symfony's `HttpClient`, and `ModelCatalog::discover()` swallows its failure, so a test without `fakeProvider()` still dials the stored base URL, and `pre_http_request` can't see it. Task 2 guards both doors in `bootstrap.php`.

   "Networking unavailable" is proved with a dead `http(s)_proxy`, not a cut network: the suite needs its database container's network.
2. **Task 3: the spec's `TerminationException('')` case is stale on the path it names.**
   - The #4311 review dropped the guard that made it a failure (#4311 comment 609), and `PipelineToolsTest.php:1079` pins it as a stop today.
   - The reachable remaining path is a toolkit throwing it from `tools()`. `AbstractAgent::run()` collects tools at `:143`, before its first `try` at `:242`, so the exception escapes into `send()`'s catch.
   - Task 3 gives that path its own sentence and pins the serial path unchanged.
3. **Task 3: residual imprecision, left alone as outside option (a).**
   - `Errors::fromPipeline()` still answers a `RunFailure` with code `alpaca_bot_provider_error`, status 502 and the fixed message "The model provider could not complete the request." Only `data.detail` loses the prefix. Renaming the code would be a wire change.
   - A consumer throwing into the generator, and the `LogicException` at `Pipeline.php:1031`, still get the prefix.
   - A plain-path provider throw with an empty message is still `Provider error: ` with nothing after it.
4. **Task 3 decision for spec review:** an announcement that was itself empty now reads "Provider error: The provider gave no reason." It used to borrow the unannounced sentence, which was wrong once option (a) makes the prefix mean "the provider threw".
5. **Task 4: when the loop actually bites.** It needs a *full* batch (≥ 100) of rows that can't be saved. Fewer end the pass on the first short batch, already skipped silently. The integration test therefore uses 100 rows. A fatal inside the loop is still not bounded, because the count comes after the loop; the docblock says so.
6. **Task 5: the fuzz harness is unrecoverable.**
   - Only the figure survives, in Kanboard #2978 comment 632.
   - The P5 ledger was gitignored in a removed worktree.
   - Nothing in `tests/`, `bin/` or git history matches "fuzz".

   Task 5 rebuilds it as a seeded differential run over 21,957 inputs, kept in scratch.
7. **Task 5 → Task 16.** Kanboard #4329 comment 617: an abandoned turn stores the raw leak, because `settle()` → `storePartial()` never reaches `recovered()`. Task 16 should decide whether the partial path recovers too. If it does, the Pipeline comment at `:283-290` ("an abandoned turn stores the raw leak") and `recovered()`'s "suppressing it live is separate" both become false and must be rewritten there.
8. **Interface naming.** `Chat\FakedToolCall` keeps Pipeline's names for its four public steps. It renames `fakedCalls()` to `calls()`, and the private `fakedPieces()` to `pieces()`, because the class name already says "faked". It adds the `OPEN` and `CLOSE` constants for Task 16's stream-side buffering.
9. **Spec §1 wording.** "`Toolkit\SummarizeToolkit` … already use both outside REST": SummarizeToolkit uses `Errors` only (`src/Toolkit/SummarizeToolkit.php:8`). `Shortcodes\Chat` and `Abilities\Register` use both. No effect on the task.
10. **Task order within Phase A.** Tasks 1, 3 and 5 all edit `src/Chat/Pipeline.php`: Task 1 one docblock at `:499`, Task 3 `send()` and `failure()`, Task 5 deletes `:611-905`. Run them in number order. Line numbers in Tasks 3 and 5 are as of `2949f14`, before the earlier tasks shift them.

```php
});

it('hands a row filter the stored value and the caller\'s own arguments, and the filter wins', function (): void {
    $access = new Access(new Store(['access.tool.web_fetch' => 'publish_posts']));
    Filters\expectApplied('alpaca_bot/capability/tool/web_fetch')->once()->with('publish_posts', 5)->andReturn('manage_options');
    expect($access->effective('tool.web_fetch', 5))->toBe('manage_options');
});

it('names each row\'s hook with its dots as slashes', function (): void {
    $access = new Access(new Store([]));
    $hooks = ['tool.summarize' => 'tool/summarize', 'tool.draft_post' => 'tool/draft_post', 'tool.abilities' => 'tool/abilities', 'mcp.github' => 'mcp/github', 'shortcode' => 'shortcode', 'settings.read' => 'settings/read', 'settings.write' => 'settings/write'];
    foreach ($hooks as $row => $hook) {
        Filters\expectApplied("alpaca_bot/capability/{$hook}")->once()->andReturn('read');
        expect($access->effective($row))->toBe('read', $row);
    }
});

it('gives the Chat row no filter of its own: its hooks are the menu\'s and each chat route\'s', function (): void {
    Filters\expectApplied('alpaca_bot/capability/chat')->never();
    $access = new Access(new Store(['access.chat' => 'read']));
    expect($access->effective('chat'))->toBe('read')
        ->and($access->overridden('chat'))->toBeFalse();
});

it('ignores a row filter that answers anything but a capability name, as Capability::filtered() does everywhere', function (): void {
    // `__return_true` is the idiom a site reaches for when a surface will not appear, and
    // `(string) true` is '1', which core reads as the legacy level_1 check.
    $access = new Access(new Store([]));
    foreach ([true, false, '1', 0, 7, '', null, ['manage_options']] as $bad) {
        Filters\expectApplied('alpaca_bot/capability/shortcode')->once()->andReturn($bad);
        expect($access->effective('shortcode'))->toBe('edit_posts', var_export($bad, true));
    }
});

it('says a row is overridden only when its filter moved it off the stored value', function (): void {
    $access = new Access(new Store(['access.tool.summarize' => 'publish_posts']));
    Filters\expectApplied('alpaca_bot/capability/tool/summarize')->once()->andReturnFirstArg();
    expect($access->overridden('tool.summarize'))->toBeFalse();
    Filters\expectApplied('alpaca_bot/capability/tool/summarize')->once()->andReturn('manage_options');
    expect($access->overridden('tool.summarize'))->toBeTrue();
    // A guarded answer is no opinion, so it overrides nothing.
    Filters\expectApplied('alpaca_bot/capability/tool/summarize')->once()->andReturn(true);
    expect($access->overridden('tool.summarize'))->toBeFalse();
});

it('asks user_can() of the user it is given, never the logged-in one', function (): void {
    $asked = [];
    Functions\when('user_can')->alias(static function (int $user, string $cap) use (&$asked): bool {
        $asked[] = [$user, $cap];
        return $user === 5;
    });
    Functions\expect('current_user_can')->never();
    $access = new Access(new Store(['access.tool.draft_post' => 'publish_posts']));
    expect($access->allows(5, 'tool.draft_post', 5))->toBeTrue()
        ->and($access->allows(6, 'tool.draft_post', 6))->toBeFalse()
        ->and($asked)->toBe([[5, 'publish_posts'], [6, 'publish_posts']]);
});
```
